# Manual checks backlog

Things only a human can do or decide, and the log of every in-game run. Claude keeps this updated.

## How to read this

- A row in the run log below is one in-game run (the game with our DLL loaded). The log runs oldest first
  (by `Run`). Each row is generated from `docs/runs/<tag>.md` (format in [`docs/lanes/README.md`](lanes/README.md))
  and carries the run's tag, its old sequential number (`Run`, cited as "run N" throughout the docs), the
  real date, the lane, the DLL, the pacing and the verdict, then the switches and the notes. Runs 1-7
  predate the table and are not listed. The latest run is the last row below.
- Verdict words: `clean` (the autotest saw no fault), `corrupt` (the autotest saw a foreign exception or other
  sign of memory damage), `crash` (the game died before a verdict), `ended` (the game shut the city down by
  itself, for example after bankruptcy), `none` (the run produced no information). A regress run reports PASS
  (both runs clean, 0 problems) or FAIL. A shadow
  line marked `INCOMPLETE` was not compared; regress counts it as a problem.
- Regress means `tools/regress.ps1` on the Konradshohe test city: a replace run (our subsystems in place of the
  originals) plus a shadows run (the originals run, and our cores are compared with them in the same process).
- Run logs are in the gitignored `build-runs/` folder, for example `build-runs/run46-smoke.log`.
- The open human checks are under "Human checks still open" below the log.

Before playing by hand, stage a build of main and reset `Plugins/FreeCity4.ini` (see `docs/ops/running-the-game.md`, "Plugins folder"), because
every harness run rewrites the ini and stages its own DLL.

Caveat: one city of 626–742 lots; attempt cost grows with candidate lots. Day data: `build-runs/run93-days.txt`. |

| glue24-rep-1-144 | 198 | 2026-10-16 | glue24 | FreeCity4-glue24 | turbo | corrupt | `enable = netmgr,netlot` (both replacements), turbo to 2032-01-01 on Konradshohe, queue request `glue24-rep-1` | **corrupt, 15 s wall, never reached the first day (julian=0).** `foreign_exceptions=1`, `ref_warnings=0`: `first-chance exception OUTSIDE FreeCity4.dll in SimCity 4.exe+0x33293C: code 0xC0000005 ACCESS_VIOLATION (reading address 0x00000000)`, right after `PostCityInit: hidden pause released`, registers `eax=0 ebp=0`, caller `SimCity 4.exe+0x8BC91`. The two null-guard fixes (a network lot with an unresolved occupant is not handed out; `GetLotConfigurationObjectArray` returns a stable empty vector instead of null) did not change it. Next: wire `create_tool` to the game's `cSC4NetworkTool` class through the GZCOM factory and load the type-0x21 exemplars into the lot core (the shadow's `FC4NetMgrShadow.cpp` already does both; copy its enumeration). |
